Phase 7 does not satisfy the mandatory close gate. I found three Critical classes of failure. Review was read-only; no tests were executed and no files were modified.

## Critical

1. **Critical — The 133-row matrix validates only that an FR label appears, not that its cited assertion exists or executes.**

   Reproduction:

   - Replace any citation in [docs/phase7-fr-matrix.md](D:/projects/workbench-kit/docs/phase7-fr-matrix.md:27) with a nonexistent ID such as `P7-NOT-A-REAL-ASSERTION`.
   - [verify-phase7.mjs](D:/projects/workbench-kit/scripts/verify-phase7.mjs:94) merely extracts the 133 SPEC IDs and searches for `| FR-x |` at lines 103–114. It never parses the “판정하는 단언” column, resolves assertion IDs, or verifies that they appear in a suite manifest.
   - The check still passes.

   There is a concrete false citation: the matrix says FR-A15 is verified by verify-phase3 §4.2 at [phase7-fr-matrix.md](D:/projects/workbench-kit/docs/phase7-fr-matrix.md:41), but that section only calls `tree.setRoot()` and `tree.clearRoot()` at [verify-phase3.mjs](D:/projects/workbench-kit/scripts/verify-phase3.mjs:364). It never checks that an upper/root-changing item, button, or key is absent.

   Violates:

   - PLAN Phase 7 lines 196–201: all checks classified, 133 cases actually checked in both hosts, all user-behavior checks passing.
   - SPEC FR-H1, NFR-5.
   - PLAN Phase 7 attack focus: nonexistent or non-running assertion IDs must not count.

2. **Critical — At least 20 “plain” FR rows are backed only by a hand-built Node mock DOM, falsely described as real-host user behavior.**

   [verify-phase3.mjs](D:/projects/workbench-kit/scripts/verify-phase3.mjs:98) explicitly calls this a “Mock DOM Environment,” defines `MockElement`, installs it as `global.document` at lines 337–352, and directly instantiates `TreeController`. No Electron or pywebview process participates.

   Nevertheless:

   - [phase7-fr-matrix.md](D:/projects/workbench-kit/docs/phase7-fr-matrix.md:14) claims these assertions come from actual keyboard/mouse simulation qualifying as user behavior.
   - [checklist.md](D:/projects/workbench-kit/docs/checklist.md:57) classifies the mock section as user-behavior testing.
   - The affected plain rows include FR-A1–A5, A8–A12, A15–A17, A19–A24, G1, and N6c. Representative direct bypasses include:
     - FR-A1: `tree.setRoot(...)`, not File → Open Folder or `Ctrl+O`, at [verify-phase3.mjs](D:/projects/workbench-kit/scripts/verify-phase3.mjs:379).
     - FR-N6c: `tree.clearRoot()`, not File → Close Folder, at line 400.
     - FR-A19: `tree.openFindWidget()` and `tree.setFindQuery()`, not `Ctrl+Alt+F` and text entry, at lines 674–682.
     - FR-A23: `tree.promptNewItem(...)`, not clicking the app-provided New File/New Folder action, at lines 864–882.
     - FR-A20/A24: fabricated button elements rather than the rendered shell titlebar, at lines 688–708.

   Bugs in host wiring, actual DOM event propagation, CSS, or the File menu can therefore pass.

   Violates SPEC’s common rule at lines 23–25, FR-H1, NFR-5, PLAN Phase 7 lines 200–201, and checklist’s own definition at lines 11–20.

3. **Critical — Several Phase 7 assertions pass independently of the required behavior.**

   Concrete cases in [phase7-suite.js](D:/projects/workbench-kit/scripts/phase7-suite.js:94):

   - `P7-FR-N3-DEFERRED` records literal `true` at lines 104–108. Window resizing can be completely broken.
   - `P7-FR-Q2` records literal `true` at lines 316–320. None of the required 3×2 rendered combinations is exercised.
   - `P7-FR-N4-MINIMIZE-CLICK` and `MAXIMIZE-CLICK` at lines 165–180 assert only that dispatching a click did not throw. A disconnected/no-op button passes without minimizing, maximizing, or restoring the window.
   - The matrix marks Q2 as a normal browser assertion at [phase7-fr-matrix.md](D:/projects/workbench-kit/docs/phase7-fr-matrix.md:152), despite the unconditional pass.

   Reproduction: remove the resize implementation, disconnect both window-button handlers, and couple the theme managers; these assertions remain green.

   Violates FR-N3, FR-N4, FR-Q2, NFR-5, and PLAN Phase 2/7 completion conditions.

4. **Critical — Other plain rows run in a real host but directly call internals instead of the FR’s UI action.**

   Representative reproducible bypasses:

   - FR-E1 switches tabs with `panel.api.setActive()` at [phase4-suite.js](D:/projects/workbench-kit/scripts/phase4-suite.js:203).
   - FR-E3 and FR-J1 close panels with `editor.closePanel()` at lines 243–245 and 567–572, bypassing `×` and `Ctrl+W`. FR-J1 literally requires both paths.
   - FR-D6/D7 call `editor.splitActiveGroup()` directly at lines 267–279 and 331–346.
   - FR-P2 drives `runActionForTest()` and `findAndReplaceForTest()` at [phase6-suite.js](D:/projects/workbench-kit/scripts/phase6-suite.js:126), bypassing rendered find-widget input.
   - FR-P3 uses `appendTextForTest()` and `triggerCommand()` at lines 140–154.
   - FR-P4 uses `appendTextForTest()` rather than keyboard input at lines 159–164.
   - FR-G3 switches tabs with `panel.api.setActive()` at lines 97–106.
   - FR-G2/G4 call `app.openFolder()` directly at lines 374–387 rather than using the folder-opening UI.
   - FR-K1–K3 in the pywebview suite simulate restart using `tree.clearRoot()`, `editor.clear()`, and `app.restoreLastSession()` in one process at lines 424–438. [verify-phase6.mjs](D:/projects/workbench-kit/scripts/verify-phase6.mjs:148) acknowledges that only Electron receives a real two-process relaunch check.
   - FR-P7 is mapped to `P6-FR-L4` at [phase7-fr-matrix.md](D:/projects/workbench-kit/docs/phase7-fr-matrix.md:149), but that assertion merely proves “Discard” closes without saving at [phase6-suite.js](D:/projects/workbench-kit/scripts/phase6-suite.js:310). It does not exercise an app raising dirty/saved state or prove shell-side saving remains zero.

   Violates the literal 판정 방법 for FR-D6, D7, E1, E3, J1, G2–G4, K1–K3, P2–P4, P7, plus FR-H1 and NFR-5.

5. **Critical — `vscode-comparison.md` hides multiple real behavioral divergences under alternate labels.**

   The document claims Zen is the only divergence, but also records:

   - VS Code Welcome View versus a completely blank Explorer at [vscode-comparison.md](D:/projects/workbench-kit/docs/vscode-comparison.md:23).
   - VS Code preview/pinned-tab behavior versus replacing the active tab at line 32. Calling tab-creation behavior an “implementation detail” is unsound: the number, identity, and persistence of visible tabs are user-observable results.
   - Dragging outside the window creates a window in VS Code but does nothing here at lines 38 and 52.
   - Default context menus in VS Code versus no menu here at lines 39 and 79.
   - VS Code’s `Ctrl+\` versus an unspecified separate shortcut at line 76.

   “Feature absent” is still a different result for the same attempted user action. NFR-3 does not exempt differences merely because they are called “의도적 배제” or “참조하되 다른 형태”.

   The validator makes this evasion possible by counting only the exact phrase `의도적 다름` at [verify-phase7.mjs](D:/projects/workbench-kit/scripts/verify-phase7.mjs:77). Any divergence under another label is invisible.

   Violates NFR-3, FR-R2, PLAN Phase 7 lines 193–195, and INTENT §6 lines 69–78.

## Major

6. **Major — File/View/Help menu tests do not prove the required rendered labels, ownership, grouping, or complete ordering.**

   At [phase7-suite.js](D:/projects/workbench-kit/scripts/phase7-suite.js:110):

   - FR-N6 compares an internal `menu.getFileItems()` list and only the first five rendered IDs. An extra shell item after the prefix, a missing/misplaced separator, or interleaved app items can pass.
   - It never counts or identifies all app-added rows separately as FR-N6 requires.
   - N6/N7/N8 compare IDs, not visible labels or shortcut text. “정보” could render as the wrong label while N8 passes.
   - `P7-FR-N5` at lines 146–150 checks only that no horizontal menu bar exists. It never asserts that the opened top-level menu groups are exactly File, View, Help; an extra fourth group passes.
   - The Phase 5 separator assertions inspect the internal menu model at [phase5-suite.js](D:/projects/workbench-kit/scripts/phase5-suite.js:367), so a DOM grouping/rendering bug still passes.

   Violates FR-N5–N8 and PLAN Phase 2 lines 56–59.

7. **Major — The icon-theme test detects class-string churn, not a visible icon change.**

   [phase7-suite.js](D:/projects/workbench-kit/scripts/phase7-suite.js:283) reads only `firstRow.className` before and after toggling.

   Reproduction:

   - Make both theme classes render the same glyph, hide `.tree-icon`, omit one font/SVG asset, or otherwise break CSS rendering while retaining different class strings.
   - `P7-FR-Q1A` still passes.
   - The round trip only proves the class string happens to match after the second render. It neither records the row’s `data-id` nor checks the active theme state or rendered glyph/image, so it cannot exclude comparing different rows or coincidental class equality.

   The selector is not the twistie—it specifically selects `.tree-icon`—and it re-queries after render, so those two narrow hazards are avoided. The missing visual proof remains.

   Violates FR-Q1/Q1a and NFR-5.

8. **Major — Zen testing uses computed style, but does not prove the complete F5/F6 result.**

   Positive point: F5 does use `getComputedStyle(...).display` at [phase7-suite.js](D:/projects/workbench-kit/scripts/phase7-suite.js:230), so it is not merely a class-name assertion.

   Missing checks:

   - F5 omits the split boundary, does not prove the tab/editor area remains visible, and never records/compar​​es the OS window’s outer size and position despite claiming it in the message.
   - F6 at lines 240–245 checks only that the Zen class is gone and the titlebar is visible. Sidebar, activity bar, statusbar, boundary, and their exact pre-Zen states are unchecked.

   Reproduction: leave the sidebar/statusbar hidden after Escape, leave pane boundaries visible during Zen, or resize/move the window on F11; the assertions can pass.

   Violates FR-F5, FR-F6, D-12, and PLAN Phase 2 lines 60–61.

9. **Major — NFR-4 checks are name-based and cover only fragments of the relevant implementation.**

   [verify-phase7.mjs](D:/projects/workbench-kit/scripts/verify-phase7.mjs:55) rejects only class names matching `*PaneManager`, `*SplitLayout`, or `*TabGroupManager`. A function-based implementation or classes named `GridCoordinator`, `WorkspaceGroups`, or similar pass.

   Likewise:

   - Monaco checks only three class-name suffixes in one file at lines 59–61.
   - Inline SVG detection examines only `src/icons/seti.ts` at lines 63–65.
   - There is no equivalent reimplementation scan for codicons or vscode-icons.
   - No check covers implementation moved to another source file.

   Violates NFR-4 and PLAN Phase 7 lines 198–199.

10. **Major — The checklist fails its own “all checks are classified” requirement.**

   [checklist.md](D:/projects/workbench-kit/docs/checklist.md:3) says it classifies `verify-dist` plus `verify-phase2~6`; it has no `verify-phase7.mjs` section at all, even though Phase 7 adds license, NFR-4, document, matrix, host-contract, and parity assertions.

   It also incorrectly classifies Phase 3’s mock DOM as user behavior at line 57 despite its own definition requiring a live Electron/pywebview browser at line 13.

   Violates FR-R3 and PLAN Phase 7 lines 196–197.

11. **Major — License and About validation permits silent legal-content drift.**

   Current repository state is internally consistent:

   - All six listed body files are present.
   - The bodies identify dockview, Monaco, seti, and code portions as MIT; codicons assets as CC BY 4.0; vscode-icons assets as CC BY-SA 4.0 plus code as MIT.
   - [about.ts](D:/projects/workbench-kit/src/core/about.ts:36) currently matches the attribution names, holders, and license families in [licenses/README.md](D:/projects/workbench-kit/licenses/README.md:7).

   But [verify-phase7.mjs](D:/projects/workbench-kit/scripts/verify-phase7.mjs:28) checks only filename existence. Empty, truncated, or wrong-license contents pass. Its About check at lines 45–48 checks four keywords, not copyright holders or consistency with README.

   Reproduction: empty `LICENSE-codicons.txt`, replace its body with another license, or change the About copyright holder while retaining `codicons`, `vscode-icons`, `CC BY 4.0`, and `CC BY-SA`; Phase 7’s checks remain green.

   Violates FR-Q4, FR-Q5, SPEC C-5, and PLAN Phase 7 lines 188–192.

## Minor

12. **Minor — Several shell assertions claim more than they inspect.**

   - `P7-FR-N1` checks element existence only, not exact counts or left/center/right placement, at [phase7-suite.js](D:/projects/workbench-kit/scripts/phase7-suite.js:82).
   - `P7-FR-N9` checks only that two elements exist and app info contains `workbench-kit`; it does not validate the actual target path, version, or Electron/pywebview branch at lines 152–157.
   - `P7-FR-M4` merely checks that some `codicon-*` class exists at lines 274–278. It does not compare glyph/path identity across three color themes or prove only color changes.
   - F1–F4 mostly inspect `.hidden` classes rather than computed visibility and never verify the required editor-width increase.

   Violates FR-N1, N9, M4, F1–F4 and their literal 판정 방법.

The new `iconTheme.toggleTheme(); tree.render();` call itself does not currently clear expansion or selection: `TreeController.render()` rebuilds DOM from the existing `expandedIds`, `selectedIds`, and `focusedId` state and restores tree focus. However, no Phase 7 assertion guards that preservation during icon-theme cycling, so a future regression there would pass.

There is no literal FR ID absent from the matrix text; the failure is stronger: many rows cite nonexistent, mock-only, unconditional, partial, or wrong-behavior evidence. Therefore the matrix cannot support FR-H1/NFR-5, and Phase 7 should remain open.
